// Soft-float double helpers: ee-gcc emits calls to these names itself for
// `double` arithmetic (see config/linker_script_extra.ld for the old aliases)

fptodp = 0x004826D8; // type:func  float -> double (e.g. float varargs)
memset = 0x0048281C; // type:func  newlib memset (the R5900 version that fills 16 bytes at a time with sq)
dptofp = 0x00481AE0; // type:func  double -> float (fp-bit df_to_sf)
sqrtf = 0x0047BFB8; // type:func  fallback ee-gcc calls for __builtin_sqrtf

// libgcc helpers ee-gcc calls by name for 64-bit arithmetic and conversions
__muldi3 = 0x00480DE0; // type:func  libgcc 64-bit multiply
__floatdidf = 0x00480D48; // type:func  libgcc s64 -> double
__fixdfdi = 0x004946F8; // type:func  libgcc double -> s64
__fixunsdfdi = 0x00494758; // type:func  libgcc double -> u64
__divdi3 = 0x00494008; // type:func  libgcc s64 /
__moddi3 = 0x00494848; // type:func  libgcc s64 %
__udivdi3 = 0x00494EB0; // type:func  libgcc u64 / (identified by elimination as the remaining one of the four)
__umoddi3 = 0x00495480; // type:func  libgcc u64 %

// Global variables with invented descriptive names. The original names are

// function's register allocation depends on the name hash (docs/progress.md),
// to hash in the order the original code needs.
